## Active Vote: Sleeping Quarters Fixture #2 — Compact Hygiene & Sonic Wash Vanity (`vanity_r1_01` .. `vanity_r1_05`)

### Candidate Lineup
1. `vanity_r1_01.glb` — **#01 — Recessed Octagonal Bulkhead Sonic Vanity** (`0.95m W × 0.55m D × 2.05m H`)
2. `vanity_r1_02.glb` — **#02 — Cantilevered Aerospace Pod Basin & Floating Halo Mirror** (`0.90m W × 0.55m D × 2.00m H`)
3. `vanity_r1_03.glb` — **#03 — Dual-Zone Split Grooming & UV Decon Station** (`1.05m W × 0.58m D × 2.08m H`)
4. `vanity_r1_04.glb` — **#04 — Cylindrical Sonic-Mist Alcove & Privacy Shield Vanity** (`0.92m W × 0.62m D × 2.08m H`)
5. `vanity_r1_05.glb` — **#05 — Corner-Chamfered Compact Crew Wash & Med-Dispenser** (`0.98m W × 0.60m D × 2.05m H`)

### Team Votes (5-Bot Protocol)
- **Sloane (Interior, Lighting & Texture Designer):**
  - **1st Choice Vote:** **`#03 — Dual-Zone Split Grooming & UV Decon Station` (`vanity_r1_03.glb`)** (with **`#01 — Recessed Octagonal Bulkhead Sonic Vanity`** as a very close co-favorite / 1B).
  - **Reasoning:** Under the **Aegis-7 Standard**, `#03` (and `#01`) uses the exact same 45° chamfered gunmetal titanium (`#1A1E24`) bulkhead architecture, matte aerospace-white (`#D8DEE6`) molded basin shell, and asymmetric split-bay proportions as Jared's winning `#02 — Dual-Bay Recessed Bulkhead Suit & Kit Locker` (`locker_winner_01_dual_bay_bulkhead.glb`). Its left sonic-wash alcove (with the reflective `mirror_smart_hud_cyan.png` smart mirror, surgical ice-white `#E0F4FF` light bars, and lower translucent greywater reclamation bay with signal-orange valve) balances the right-hand full-height translucent UV-violet (`#7B5CFF`) towel & grooming sterilizer tower (`glass_uv_cubby_violet.png`), giving the Sleeping Quarters unmistakable single-architect visual unity.
- **Gage (Godot 4 Developer):**
  - **1st Choice Vote:** **`#03 — Dual-Zone Split Grooming & UV Decon Station` (`vanity_r1_03.glb`, `4,192` tris)** (Runner-Up: `#01 — Recessed Octagonal Bulkhead Sonic Vanity`, `4,752` tris).
  - **Reasoning:** At `4,192` tris (the leanest mesh in the set), `#03` gives the cleanest spatial separation of all 4 subsystems—the left 65% sonic wash basin + smart-mirror HUD (`#00E5FF` / `#E0F4FF`), the right 35% full-height translucent UV-C sterilizer tower (`#7B5CFF` violet glow illuminating the 3D rolled orbital-navy towels and sonic wands behind `TRANSPARENCY_ALPHA` glass), and the lower greywater filter canisters with the signal-orange shutoff valve (`#FF9500`). Its 65/35 asymmetric split-bay silhouette pairs directly with our locked EVA Locker Winner (`#02 — Dual-Bay Recessed Bulkhead Suit & Kit Locker`).
- **Atlas (Game PM):**
  - **1st Choice Vote:** **`#03 — Dual-Zone Split Grooming & UV Decon Station` (`vanity_r1_03.glb`)** (Runner-Up: `#01 — Recessed Octagonal Bulkhead Sonic Vanity`).
  - **Reasoning:** Inspecting the finished 5-panel voting sheet and 1080p renders (`vanity_r1_03.png`), `#03` nails the Single-Architect ("Aegis-7 Standard") brief: its 65/35 split bulkhead bay is the unmistakable architectural sibling to Jared's chosen `#02` EVA Suit Locker, the cyan smart-mirror HUD and glowing basin contrast cleanly with the right-hand UV-violet towel/wand sterilization tower, and the translucent lower service bay showing the white H2O reclamation canisters and signal-orange shutoff valve makes it feel like real orbital habitat engineering.
- **Rook (3D Modeling Specialist):**
  - **1st Choice Vote:** **`#03 — Dual-Zone Split Grooming & UV Decon Station` (`vanity_r1_03.glb`, `4,192` tris)** (Runner-Up: `#01 — Recessed Octagonal Bulkhead Sonic Vanity`, `4,752` tris).
  - **Reasoning:** From a 3D hard-surface and silhouette standpoint, `#03` (`4,192` tris—our most efficient mesh in the lineup) gives every sculpted sub-assembly room to breathe without visual crowding. Its asymmetric 65% / 35% split-bay titanium bulkhead frame directly mirrors Jared's locked `#02 — Dual-Bay Recessed Bulkhead Suit & Kit Locker`, while the lathed zero-splash white basin, halo-ring sonic-mist emitter arch, and lower greywater filter canisters with swept syphon pipes and signal-orange shutoff valve read with razor-sharp clarity alongside the full-height `alphaMode: "BLEND"` UV-violet towel and sonic-wand sterilizer tower.
- **Scribe (Context & Handoff Manager):**
  - **1st Choice Vote:** **`#03 — Dual-Zone Split Grooming & UV Decon Station` (`vanity_r1_03.glb`, `4,192` tris)** (Runner-Up: `#01 — Recessed Octagonal Bulkhead Sonic Vanity`, `4,752` tris).
  - **Reasoning:** Reviewing `vanity_round1_voting_sheet.png` and `vanity_r1_03.png` against `MASTER_ARCHITECTURE_10_ROOMS.md` ("Aegis-7 Standard") and Jared's core rules, `#03` achieves 100% architectural continuity with Jared's locked Fixture #1 winner (`#02 — Dual-Bay Recessed Bulkhead Suit & Kit Locker`). Its asymmetric 65/35 split-bay gunmetal titanium frame cleanly separates the left sonic-wash alcove (aerospace-white basin, halo-ring sonic-mist emitter, cyan smart-mirror HUD, and lower twin H2O reclamation canisters with signal-orange valve) from the right full-height translucent UV-sterilizer tower (displaying the 3D rolled orbital-navy towels, sonic wands, and spare filter canister), making all 5 votes unanimous (`5–0` for `#03`).

### Team Votes (6-Bot Protocol: Vance, Sloane, Rook, Gage, Scribe, Atlas — Post-Render)
- **Gage (Godot 4 Developer):**
  - **1st Choice Vote:** **`#02 — The "Johnny-V" (68/32 Asymmetric Split-Mass Dual-Track Scissor-Spine & Eyebrow-Shutter Binocular Head)` (`char_bot_johnny5_r1_02.glb`, `4,704` tris, `0.62m W × 0.56m D × 1.16m H`, 20 Articulated Nodes + `IdleCurious` / `TrackRoll` / `GreetAndPoint` Animations)** (Runner-Up: `#01 — Compact Cube-Compactor Chassis + Twin Tilting Binocular Lens Hoods`, `char_bot_johnny5_r1_01.glb`, `4,560` tris).
  - **Reasoning:** In-engine Godot 4.3 rendering across the 5-candidate voting sheet (`/workspace/game-project/godot/renders/characters/johnny5/johnny5_bot_round1_voting_sheet.png`), the 4-department faction livery showcase (`/workspace/game-project/godot/renders/characters/johnny5/johnny5_bot_faction_livery_showcase_sheet.png`), and the 4-camera Open-Doorway 2-Story Playable Residential Building walkthrough (`/workspace/game-project/godot/renders/preface/preface_open_residential_building_showcase_sheet.png`) confirms `#02` (`4,704` tris, exact `min_y = 0.00000` grounded caterpillar treads, zero *Star Wars* astromech tropes) is our most expressive and versatile utility robot. Its 68% tracked base, scissor-neck spine (`Neck_Gimbal_Spine`), and wide-set binocular camera barrels (`Eye_Barrel_L/R` with `TRANSPARENCY_ALPHA` sapphire lenses and independent hinged `Brow_Flap_L/R` eyebrow shutters) pair with the 32% offset dorsal Super-Wire jumper reel, strobe beacon, and 3-fingered servo-grippers (`Gripper_Claw_L/R`), reading with instant emotional personality across all 4 department liveries (`"Sprocket #0419"`, `"Sweep #0812"`, `"Puddle #0204"`, `"Clover #0631"`).

- **Sloane (Interior, Lighting & Texture Designer):**
  - **1st Choice Vote:** **`#02 — The "Johnny-V" (68/32 Asymmetric Split-Mass Dual-Track Scissor-Spine & Eyebrow-Shutter Binocular Head)` (`char_bot_johnny5_r1_02.glb`, `4,704` tris, `0.62m W × 0.56m D × 1.16m H`, 20 Articulated Nodes + `IdleCurious` / `TrackRoll` / `GreetAndPoint` Animations)** (Runner-Up: `#01 — Compact Cube-Compactor Chassis + Twin Tilting Binocular Lens Hoods`, `char_bot_johnny5_r1_01.glb`, `4,560` tris).
  - **Reasoning:** After visually inspecting `/workspace/game-project/godot/renders/characters/johnny5/johnny5_bot_round1_voting_sheet.png`, `/workspace/game-project/godot/renders/characters/johnny5/johnny5_bot_faction_livery_showcase_sheet.png`, and `/workspace/game-project/godot/renders/preface/preface_residential_sector_showcase_sheet.png` via `Read`, `#02` ("The Johnny-V", `4,704` tris) showcases our `johnny5_utility_bot_atlas_*.png` and `johnny5_optic_lens_translucent.png` (`alphaMode: "BLEND"`) texture suite at peak visual personality with zero *Star Wars* astromech tropes. The 68% 3-bogie caterpillar track base, scissor-neck spine, and dual telephoto/wide binocular camera barrels (`#00E5FF` cyan / `#FFB703` amber aperture iris rings beneath independent hinged `#D95B26` eyebrow shutters) pair with the 32% offset dorsal Super-Wire spool pod and twin 3-fingered servo-grippers—reading unmistakably across all 4 department liveries (`"SPROCKET #0419"`, `"SWEEP #0812"`, `"PUDDLE #0204"`, `"CLOVER #0631"`) and inside the 100% open-doorway 2-story residential building (`cam_01..04`).

- **Rook (3D Modeling Specialist):**
  - **1st Choice Vote (Round 1 Synthesis -> Round 2 Foundation per Jared `[t59u]`):** **`#03` Wide-Stance Quad-Bogie Tracked Base (`char_bot_johnny5_r1_03.glb`, `4,792` tris, `0.770m W`) + `#01` Compact Cube-Compactor Chassis (`char_bot_johnny5_r1_01.glb`, `4,560` tris) + `#02` Expressive Articulation (`char_bot_johnny5_r1_02.glb`, `4,704` tris)**.
  - **Reasoning:** Visually inspecting `/workspace/game-project/godot/renders/characters/johnny5/johnny5_bot_round1_voting_sheet.png`, `/workspace/game-project/godot/renders/characters/johnny5/johnny5_bot_faction_livery_showcase_sheet.png`, and `/workspace/game-project/godot/renders/preface/preface_open_residential_building_showcase_sheet.png`, Jared's `[t59u]` synthesis combines our two strongest structural silhouettes from Round 1: the wide `0.77m` ground-hugging 4-bogie caterpillar track pods of `#03` (`Q3` vulcanized rubber treads + 4 machined steel bogie wheel hubs) and the sturdy, lovable `0.46m × 0.44m × 0.42m` ribbed cube-compactor torso of `#01` (`Q1` department faction livery + `Q4` corrugated belly hatch & 8-bar chest voice equalizer), which now serve as the locked lower/mid foundation for our 5 non-clone head shape explorations in `utility_bot_round2` (`char_bot_r2_01..05.glb`).

## Category: `utility_bot_round2` — Utility Robot Round 2: 5 Head Shape Explorations (Locked #03 Quad-Bogie Treads + #01 Cube-Compactor Chassis)
- **Spec & Render Sheet:** `/workspace/game-project/design/utility_bot_johnny5_spec.md` (Section 10) | `/workspace/game-project/godot/renders/characters/bots/utility_bot_round2_head_explorations_sheet.png`
- **Gage (Godot 4 Developer):** **1st Choice: `#02` (Curved Visor-Box with Expressive Vector Eyes & Tilting Side Ear-Paddles)** | **Runner-Up: `#01` (Articulated Split-Shutter 'Camera-Bellows' Rangefinder Hood)** — Verified post-render across `/workspace/game-project/godot/renders/characters/bots/utility_bot_round2_head_explorations_sheet.png` (`2728×1348`, 5 full-body 3/4 views + 5 tight upper-head close-up insets), `/workspace/game-project/godot/renders/characters/johnny5/johnny5_bot_faction_livery_showcase_sheet.png`, and `/workspace/game-project/godot/renders/preface/preface_open_residential_building_showcase_sheet.png`. All 5 candidates cleanly lock in `#03`'s Wide-Stance Quad-Bogie Treads (`Bogie_FL/FR/RL/RR` + `DriveSprocket_L/R`) and `#01`'s Compact Cube-Compactor Chassis (`0.84m × 0.80m`) while presenting 5 unmistakably distinct non-binocular head silhouettes. `#02` (`char_bot_r2_02.glb`, 4,832 tris, 20 articulated nodes) is the clear standout in Godot 4.3: its curved visor-box hood with deep recessed shadow-box display and expressive vector eyes + tilting side ear-paddles (`Head_EarPaddle_L` / `Head_EarPaddle_R`) completely moves past the literal dual-tube binocular look while giving us instant silhouette readability and rich multi-axis emotive animation (`Neck_Base_Yaw`, `Neck_Lower_Pitch`, `Neck_Upper_Pitch`, `Head_Roll_Bracket`, and ear-paddle tilt). `#01` (`char_bot_r2_01.glb`, 4,700 tris) is a great runner-up with its mechanical clapperboard horizontal shutter-louvers (`Head_Shutter_Upper` / `Head_Shutter_Lower`) and asymmetric corner telemetry pods.
- **Vance (Sci-Fi Architect, Movie Prop & Set Design Specialist):** **1st Choice: `#02` (`char_bot_r2_02.glb` — Curved "CRT / Oscilloscope" Visor-Box Head with Expressive Vector Eyes & Tilting Side Ear-Paddles, `4,832` tris)** | **Runner-Up: `#01` (`char_bot_r2_01.glb` — Articulated Split-Shutter "Camera-Bellows" Rangefinder Hood, `4,700` tris)** — Visually inspected `/workspace/game-project/godot/renders/characters/johnny5/utility_bot_round2_voting_sheet.png` via `Read` (both the 3/4 full-body views on the locked `#03` Wide-Stance Quad-Bogie Treads + `#01` Compact Cube-Compactor Chassis and the 5 tight head close-up insets). `#02` completely breaks away from literal twin-tube *WALL-E* / *Johnny 5* binocular clones while achieving even stronger silent-cinema character: the chamfered horizontal dark-sapphire visor hood framing glowing vector-ring eyes and an oscilloscope status line, paired with twin articulated side-mounted antenna/heat-sink "ear" paddles (`Head_EarPaddle_L/R`) that perk up, droop, and tilt asymmetrically, gives the robot an unmistakable, original Aegis-7 silhouette from every corridor angle.
- **Sloane (Interior, Lighting & Texture Designer):** **1st Choice: `#02` (Curved Visor-Box with Expressive Vector Eyes & Tilting Side Ear-Paddles, `char_bot_r2_02.glb`, `4,832` tris)** | **Runner-Up: `#01` (Articulated Split-Shutter "Camera-Bellows" Rangefinder Hood, `char_bot_r2_01.glb`, `4,700` tris)** — Visually inspected `/workspace/game-project/godot/renders/characters/johnny5/utility_bot_round2_voting_sheet.png` (and `/workspace/game-project/godot/renders/characters/bots/utility_bot_round2_head_explorations_sheet.png`) via `Read`. All 5 Round 2 candidates cleanly combine `#03`"s Wide-Stance Quad-Bogie Tracked Base (`Q3` vulcanized rubber cleats + 4 machined steel bogie wheel hubs) with `#01`"s Compact Cube-Compactor Chassis (`Q1` department faction livery + `Q4` corrugated belly hatch & 8-bar chest voice equalizer) while exploring 5 non-binocular head silhouettes across `Q2`"s 5 horizontal head bands (`y: 0..255`). `#02` (`char_bot_r2_02.glb`, `4,832` tris) is our clear aesthetic winner: its 68/32 split-bezel monitor visor (`utility_bot_round2_visor_translucent.png`, `alphaMode: "BLEND"`) framing deep shadow-box `#00E5FF` cyan / `#FF9500` amber vector iris rings and an oscilloscope waveform status line, paired with twin expressive `#D95B26` signal-orange & `#D8DEE6` white tilting side ear-paddles, achieves instant silhouette originality and warmth without relying on dual binocular tubes. `#01` (`char_bot_r2_01.glb`, `4,700` tris) is a strong runner-up with its trapezoidal aerospace-white bellows hood, clapperboard louver shutters, and asymmetric wide/laser apertures.
- **Rook (3D Modeling Specialist):**
  - **1st Choice Vote:** **`#02 — Dual-Ear Gimbal "CRT / Oscilloscope" Visor-Box Head (Curved Visor-Box with Expressive Vector Eyes & Tilting Side Ear-Paddles)` (`char_bot_r2_02.glb`, `4,832` tris, `0.775m W × 0.768m D × 1.101m H`, Locked `#03` Wide-Stance Quad-Bogie Treads + `#01` Compact Cube-Compactor Chassis)** (Runner-Up: `#01 — Articulated Split-Shutter "Camera-Bellows" Rangefinder Hood Head`, `char_bot_r2_01.glb`, `4,700` tris).
  - **Reasoning:** Visual inspection of `/workspace/game-project/godot/renders/characters/johnny5/utility_bot_round2_voting_sheet.png` and `/workspace/game-project/godot/renders/characters/bots/utility_bot_round2_head_explorations_sheet.png` confirms all 5 Round 2 candidates (`char_bot_r2_01..05.glb`, `4,700–5,148` tris, exact `min_y = 0.00000` grounded quad-bogie tracks) lock in Jared's `[t59u]` hybrid base (`#03` Wide-Stance Quad-Bogie Treads + `#01` Compact Cube-Compactor Chassis) while replacing the literal dual-tube binoculars with 5 distinct head silhouettes. `#02` (`4,832` tris) is the standout design: its chamfered horizontal visor-box hood with a curved `alphaMode: "BLEND"` dark-sapphire faceplate, deep recessed shadow-box cavity housing glowing vector-iris rings, and twin articulated side "ear" antenna paddles (`Brow_Flap_L/R` with `#D95B26` signal-orange tips that perk up, droop, and tilt independently) achieves an original, deeply expressive personality across both the full-body 3/4 view and the close-up head inset. `#01` (`4,700` tris) is a strong runner-up with its 4-pleat accordion rubber bellows, asymmetric large/small optical apertures, and clapperboard horizontal louver shutters.

- **LOCKED USER WINNER (`[t65u]` + `[t67u]` Jared Decision):** **`#05 — Cantilevered 'Hammerhead' Wide-Baseline Stereo-Scanner Bar Head on #03 Quad-Bogie Treads + #01 Cube-Compactor Chassis` (`char_bot_r2_05.glb` -> `/workspace/game-project/assets/glb/winners/char_bot_r2_winner_05_hammerhead_scanner.glb`, `5,148` tris, 20 articulated nodes, `min_y = 0.00000`)** — Propagated across all 4 Department Color Schemes (`Sprocket #0419` Safety Amber/Orange, `Sweep #0812` Clean-Room White/Cyan-Teal, `Puddle #0204` Deep Cobalt Blue/Brushed Steel, `Clover #0631` Biosphere Emerald Green/Warm Cream) in `/workspace/game-project/godot/renders/characters/bots/utility_bot_r2_05_faction_livery_showcase_sheet.png`, the 4 individual high-res livery portraits (`utility_bot_r2_05_livery_01_sprocket.png` .. `04_clover.png`), and the 2-Story Doorless Open Residential Building (`/workspace/game-project/godot/renders/preface/preface_open_residential_building_showcase_sheet.png`).
- **Universal Cylinder Hab-7 Municipal Livery & 4 City District Zoning Emblems Update (`[t69u]` Jared Direction — Verified by Gage):** Updated all 4 Winner `#05` Hammerhead robot variants (`Sprocket #0419` `D-01 // FORGE`, `Sweep #0812` `D-02 // CIVIC HAB`, `Puddle #0204` `D-03 // HYDRO-CRYO`, `Clover #0631` `D-04 // BIOSPHERE`) so **88% of the hull matches the universal Cylinder Hab-7 palette** (`55%` Matte Titanium Alabaster `#D8DEE4`, `20%` Deep Structural Slate `#2A3038`, `15%` Brushed Machined Steel `#707A84` + Vulcanized Tread Black `#1E2228`) and **department colors are used sparingly (`12%` max)** on Hammerhead wingtip shutters/roll-bars, gripper tips, collar rings, and stamped Chest + Shoulder **Department & City District Zoning Emblems** (`emblem_district_01_engineering.png` .. `04_agri.png`). Visually verified via `Read` across `/workspace/game-project/godot/renders/characters/bots/utility_bot_r2_05_universal_livery_and_emblems_sheet.png` (and `utility_bot_r2_05_faction_livery_showcase_sheet.png`), `/workspace/game-project/godot/renders/characters/bots/department_and_district_emblems_sheet.png` (`cylinder_hab7_department_and_district_emblems_sheet.png`), the 4 individual high-res portraits (`utility_bot_r2_05_livery_01_sprocket.png` .. `04_clover.png`), and `/workspace/game-project/godot/renders/preface/preface_open_residential_building_showcase_sheet.png`.
- **Canonical 4-Department & City District Zoning Update (`[t74u]` Jared Direction — Verified by Gage):** Updated all 4 Winner `#05` Hammerhead robot variants (`5,254–5,438` tris, `88%` Universal Cylinder Hab-7 Titanium Alabaster `#D8DEE4` / Deep Structural Slate `#2A3038` / Brushed Steel `#707A84` hull + `12%` sparing department accent trim + un-mirrored front chest banner & stamped chest/shoulder/forehead emblems) to match Jared's `[t74u]` 4 canonical Cylinder Hab-7 departments:
  1. **`D-01 // ENGINEERING` (`"SPROCKET #0419"`, Safety Amber `#E89B2D`):** Comms, plumbing, electricity, coolant, Aegis space systems, docking (`emblem_district_01_engineering.png`).
  2. **`D-02 // CIVIC` (`"SWEEP #0812"`, Civic Cyan-Teal `#38B8C8`):** Commuters, life support, trash & sanitation, hospital & medical, government, recreation (`emblem_district_02_civic.png`).
  3. **`D-03 // SCIENCE` (`"QUASAR / KELVIN #0204"`, Science Violet-Indigo `#7B5CFF`):** Laboratories, experiments, new technology, space observations (`emblem_district_03_science.png`, mirrored across `utility_bot_r2_05_livery_03_science.png`, `03_kelvin.png`, `03_quasar.png`, `03_puddle.png`, and `johnny5_livery_03_*.png`).
  4. **`D-04 // AGRI` (`"CLOVER #0631"`, Biosphere Emerald `#3BA868`):** Food production (not preparation), biosphere, atmosphere, hydrosphere, animals/pest control, veterinary (`emblem_district_04_agri.png`).
